Newton sqrt algorithm:
Given polynomial D of length n with D0=1. Want B length n with B^2 ≡ D mod z^n, B0=1.
Initialize B=[1]. For m doubling 1,2,4,...<n:
  Compute B^2 mod z^{2m}: C = convolution(B,B)[:2m]
  err = D[:2m] - C
  B_new = B + err / (2B) mod z^{2m}; but need inverse of B mod z^{2m}.

Implement poly sqrt using Newton. Let's design top-level functions:
```
def _fft_nextpow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _poly_mul_trunc(x,y,L):
    n=len(x); m=len(y); if n==0 or m==0: return zeros
    r = _fft_nextpow2(n+m-1)
    X=np.fft.fft(x, r); Y=np.fft.fft(y, r)
    out=np.fft.ifft(X*Y)[:L].real
    return out

def _poly_sqrt_series(D, length):
    D=np.asarray(D, dtype=float)[:length].copy()
    if D[0] <= small: ...
    D = D / D[0]
    B = np.ones(1)
    inv = np.ones(1)
    m=1
    while m < length:
        L= min(2*m, length)
        # inverse update for B to length L
        # bL = extend B to L
        b_pad = np.zeros(L); b_pad[:m]=B
        inv_pad = zeros(L); inv_pad[:m]=inv
        # inv_new = inv_pad * (2 - b_pad*inv_pad) truncated L
        b_inv_mul = _poly_mul_trunc(b_pad, inv_pad, L)
        inv_new = _poly_mul_trunc(inv_pad, 2.0 - b_inv_mul, L)
        inv = inv_new
        # B squared truncated using current B? Or use inv_new and D?
        b2 = _poly_mul_trunc(b_pad, b_pad, L)  # current B
        err = np.zeros(L); err[:L] = D[:L] - b2
        b_pad_new = _poly_mul_trunc(inv_new, err, L)
        B_new = b_pad + 0.5*b_pad_new
        # ensure first m unchanged? To avoid instability, set B_new[:m]=B? Newton should. But due numerical, maybe.
        B_new[:m] = B
        B = B_new
        m = L
    return B * sqrt(D_original[0])
```
Need check inverse Newton: Given inv such that B*inv=1 mod m. To extend to L=2m, inv_new = inv*(2 - B*inv) mod L. Uses B length L? B known only m but higher zeros for update; that's okay because Newton inverse from m to 2m uses old B and inv; high coefficients of B in 2-B*inv maybe? Standard formula: inv_{2m} = inv_m (2 - B_{2m} inv_m) mod z^{2m}. B_{2m} should include new coefficients of B, which we don't have before updating inverse. But since inv_m is exact to m and B_m exact to m, using B padded with zeros for high degrees yields inverse to 2m? Let's verify: B = B_m + z^m B_hi. inv_m exact means B inv_m =1+O(z^m). However in sqrt Newton, we can update B and inv simultaneously using only B to m? The standard iteration for reciprocal of a series known to m: x_{n+1}=x_n(2 - a x_n) gives reciprocal to 2m using the full known coefficients of a to 2m? I think you need a to 2m. But in sqrt formula, B_new = (B + D/B)/2; to compute D/B to 2m, need inverse of B to 2m, which requires B to 2m. Top-level:
```
def _poly_mul(a,b,L):
    na=len(a); nb=len(b)
    need=na+nb-1
    if need<=0: return np.zeros(L)
    r=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, r)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b, r)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*B, r)[:L]
    return out
```
If L smaller than need, still compute full inverse then truncate; could use next pow2 of L? But product coefficients beyond L not needed; can't just convolve length L? If input lengths longer than L, we can truncate each to L before multiply because high coefficients contribute only degree ≥L. Then need = min(len(aa)+len(bb)-1, L) for r? We need compute via FFT result first L, choose r next pow2 of min(need, L?) ```
def _poly_sqrt_positive_first(C):
    C = np.asarray(C,float)
    n=len(C)
    if n==0: return None
    c0=C[0]
    if not np.isfinite(c0) or c0 <= 1e-300:
       # add tiny?
    scale = np.sqrt(c0)
    D = C.copy()/c0
    D = D[:n]
    B = np.array([1.0])
    inv = np.array([1.0])
    m=1
    while m<n:
       L=min(2*m,n)
       # pad current B and inv to L
       b_old = np.zeros(L); b_old[:m]=B
       inv_old = np.zeros(L); inv_old[:m]=inv
       # compute tmp_inv = inv_old * (2 - b_old*inv_old) mod L
       b_inv = _poly_mul(b_old, inv_old, L)
       two = np.zeros(L); two[0]=2.0
       rhs = two - b_inv
       tmp_inv = _poly_mul(inv_old, rhs, L)
       # correction err = D_L - b_old^2
       b2 = _poly_mul(b_old, b_old, L)
       dL = np.zeros(L); dL[:n]=D  # D length n
       err = dL - b2
       corr = _poly_mul(tmp_inv, err, L)
       B_new = b_old + 0.5*corr
       # update inv to inverse of B_new mod L
       b_new_inv_old = _poly_mul(B_new, tmp_inv, L)
       rhs2 = two - b_new_inv_old
       inv = _poly_mul(tmp_inv, rhs2, L)
       B = B_new
       m=L
    a = B * scale
    return a
```

We can implement a loop:
```
def c_cap_root_improve(a, max_iters=100, ratios=[0.95,0.9,0.8], n_active_frac=0.2):
  best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
  for it in range(max_iters):
    c=conv(a); M=max(c); level=M*ratio (maybe adaptive)
    active = c > level
    d_full=c.copy(); d_full[active]=level
    # Maybe we should smooth d_full to avoid sharp cap causing negative root: blend d = (1-β)c + β d_cap
    C_tar=d_full[:n]
    a_new = poly_sqrt(C_tar); clip; normalize; sc
    if sc < best_sc - tol: accept a_new, continue with smaller ratio?
    else try lower beta or break.
```

Function to propose:
```
def _c_cap_step(a, invB, C, M, ratio, active_tol, beta):
    n=len(a)
    a0=a[0]
    # base C normalized D? using C/a0^2
    # active on full C but first half
    level = M * ratio  # ratio<1
    # perhaps target: where C >= level? cap to level; or active top where C > M*(1-active_tol)
    C_first = C[:n]
    active = C_first >= level  # if ratio .95
    # To exclude k=0? if active[0] True because C0? C0 smaller usually. But if not, we can set active[0]=False to keep a0.
    if n>1: active[0]=False
    if not np.any(active): return None
    dC = np.zeros(n)
    dC[active] = level - C_first[active]  # <=0
    # scale factor beta
    dD = dC / (a0*a0)
    # deltaB = 0.5 * conv(invB, dD) mod n
    deltaB = 0.5 * _poly_mul(invB, dD, n)
    B = a / a0
    B_new = B + beta * deltaB
    B_new[0]=1.0  # enforce
    a_new = a0 * B_new
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    a_new /= s
    return a_new
```
Actually `_poly_mul(invB,dD,n)` returns convolution truncated n. If invB and dD length n, product length 2n-1; first n. Good. dD could be large if a0 small; but okay.

Let's define reciprocal functions:
```
def _reciprocal_series(B, length, full=True):
    # B[0] != 0; assume B0=1? If not, scale internally.
    b=B.copy(); if b[0]!=1: b=b/b[0]; scale? But we use B0=1.
    inv=np.array([1.0])
    m=1
    while m<length:
       L=min(2*m,length)
       b_pad=zeros(L); b_pad[:m]=b[:m]
       inv_pad=zeros(L); inv_pad[:m]=inv
       t=_poly_mul(b_pad, inv_pad, L)
       rhs=zeros(L); rhs[0]=2.0; rhs -= t
       inv=_poly_mul(inv_pad, rhs, L)
       m=L
    if B0 not 1: inv /= B0? Actually inverse of B is (1/B0)*inverse of B/B0. If B0 not 1. We'll require B0=1.
    return inv
```
This computes inverse of B0=1. In update, use B length n, inv current length n. One Newton step:
```
def _reciprocal_newton_step(B, inv, length, n_steps):
    # B, inv length length? pad to length
    for _ in range(n_steps):
       b_pad = B[:length]
       inv_pad = inv[:length]
       t=np.poly_mul(b_pad, inv_pad, length)
       rhs=zeros(length); rhs[0]=2; rhs-=t
       inv=np.poly_mul(inv_pad, rhs, length)
    return inv
```
If B0=1. If B0 not exactly due normalization? set B0=1.

Implement phase `linear_root_polish(a, time_limit, max_iters)`:
- normalize, compute C, M, score.
- if score nan, return.
- compute B=a/a0 if a0>eps else return.
- inv=_reciprocal_series(B,n)
- full_inverse_period = 10
- ratios list; maybe dynamic start with [0.99,0.98,0.95,0.9]
- betas line [0.2,0.5,1.0] plus scaled.
- For iteration up to max_iters/time:
   best_improved=False
   for ratio in ratios:
      if time limit break
      level=M*ratio
      first=C[:n]
      active = first > level  # strict
      if n>1: active[0]=False
      # focus on top peaks: maybe require first >= M*(1-active_frac) and > level? Could use active = (first - level)>1e-12.
      if no active: continue
      dC = zeros(n); dC[active] = level - first[active]
      dD = dC/(a0*a0)
      # maybe add small average to dD? Not necessary; scale invariant but if only negative, B_new may sum lower; normalized. Okay.
      deltaB = 0.5*_poly_mul(inv, dD, n)
      # deltaB[0] should be 0 if active excludes 0; set 0.
      delta_max = np.max(np.abs(deltaB[1:])) if n>1 else 0
      if delta_max < 1e-15: continue
      scale = min(1.0, 0.1 / delta_max)  # cap per coefficient change relative maybe
      for beta in [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0]:
         b= beta*scale
         B_new = B + b*deltaB
         B_new[0]=1.0
         a_new = a0 * B_new
         if not finite or min < -0.5*max? clip
         a_new_clipped = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
         s=...
         if s<=0 continue
         a_try = a_new_clipped/s
         sc = compute_score(a_try)
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             accept; updated=True; break beta
      if improved: break ratio? Could try one ratio per iter; use ratio=0.99 small. To converge, choose ratio = 0.995 maybe. Simpler: use ratios in increasing aggressiveness until improvement; after improvement, restart with smallest or keep.
   if no improvement: break
- Accept line: update a, C, M, B, inv.
   a0=a[0]; if a0<eps: recompute or break. B=a/a0; B[0]=1.
   inv = _reciprocal_newton_step(B, inv, n, steps=2)
   recompute_counter++
   if counter%10==0: inv=_reciprocal_series(B,n) (full)
   # check inv residual? maybe if max abs inv >1e9: full or break.
```

Functions:
```
def _poly_mul(a,b,L):
   if len(a)==0 or len(b)==0: return zeros(L)
   # truncate inputs to L
   if len(a)>L: a=a[:L]
   if len(b)>L: b=b[:L]
   need=len(a)+len(b)-1
   if need<=0: return zeros
   r=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, r)
   B=np.fft.rfft(b, r)
   out=np.fft.irfft(A*B, r)
   if L < out.size: out=out[:L]
   else: out=np.pad(out,(0,L-out.size)) # though need>=L? not necessarily but r ensures len r >= need, out size r; if r may >L.
   return out
```
For performance, if L small and need small (<64), use np.convolve? Simpler always FFT; for small overhead maybe. Root step L=n large; inverse updates use smaller L in full reciprocal. For full reciprocal many small L; FFT for small might be overhead. We can implement direct for need <= 64:
```
if need <= 64:
   out=np.convolve(a,b)[:L]
else FFT
```
Top-level no lambda. Good.

But `_poly_mul` in reciprocal at small L many calls; direct faster. Use threshold 128.

Need ensure arrays float64.

`_reciprocal_full(B, length)`:
```
B = np.asarray(B,float).copy()
if B[0]==0 or not finite: return None
scale=1.0/B[0]
b=B*scale
inv=np.array([1.0])
m=1
while m<length:
   L=min(2*m,length)
   b_pad=np.zeros(L); b_pad[:m]=b[:m]
   inv_pad=np.zeros(L); inv_pad[:m]=inv
   t=_poly_mul(b_pad, inv_pad, L)
   rhs=np.zeros(L); rhs[0]=2.0; rhs[t size?] subtract
   # t length L
   rhs = rhs - t
   inv = _poly_mul(inv_pad, rhs, L)
   m=L
# if B0 not 1? b scaled, inverse of b; inverse of B = inv * B[0]? Since b=B/B0, 1/B = B0 * 1/b. But we use B0=1. If not, return inv * B[0]. For B normalized B0=1.
return inv
```
Check: if b=B/B0, inverse of b is invb; inverse of B = (1/B0)*invb. Since B0=1, okay.

`_reciprocal_step(B, inv, length, n_steps)`:

for _ in range(n_steps):
   t=_poly_mul(B, inv, length)
   rhs=np.zeros(length); rhs[0]=2.0; rhs-=t
   inv=_poly_mul(inv, rhs, length)
return inv
```

```
def _linear_root_polish(a, time_limit, max_iters=1000):
   a,s_ok=_normalize_nonnegative(a)
   if not s_ok: return a, -inf
   n=len(a)
   C=self_conv(a); M=C.max(); sc=2*n*M
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
   if n<2 or a[0]<=1e-300: return best_a,best_sc
   B=a/a[0]; B[0]=1.0
   inv=_reciprocal_full(B,n)
   if inv is None or not finite or max(abs)>1e100: return
   ratios = [0.995,0.99,0.98,0.96,0.93,0.9]
   betas = [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]
   recompute_every=8
   counter=0
   no_work=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>time_limit: break
      # Recompute C/M if needed? Keep.
      improved=False
      # Try ratios maybe dynamic: start small; if no improvement, loop.
      for ratio in ratios:
          if time.monotonic()>time_limit: break
          level=M*ratio
          first=C[:n]
          active = first > level
          if n>1:
             active[0]=False
             # maybe if no active, try top 1? but if only center, active true if center > level (yes)
          if not active.any(): continue
          dC=np.zeros(n)
          dC[active]=level - first[active]
          # optional: limit dC magnitude? no.
          dD=dC/(a[0]*a[0])
          # If dD has nan/inf, skip
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(dD)): continue
          deltaB=0.5*_poly_mul(inv,dD,n)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(deltaB)): continue
          deltaB[0]=0.0
          delta_max=float(np.max(np.abs(deltaB))) if deltaB.size else 0.0
          if delta_max <= 1e-15: continue
          scale_global=min(1.0, 0.25/delta_max)  # adapt
          best_candidate=None; best_candidate_sc=best_sc
          for beta in betas:
             b=beta*scale_global
             if b<=0: continue
             B_new=B + b*deltaB
             B_new[0]=1.0
             a_new=a[0]*B_new
             # if many negative? clip; but maybe reject if >20% negative? 
             if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)): continue
             a_clip=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
             s=float(a_clip.sum())
             if s<=1e-300: continue
             a_try=a_clip/s
             sc_try=compute_score(a_try)
             if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 best_sc=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); best_candidate=a_try.copy(); improved=True; break
          if improved:
              break
      if improved:
          a=best_a
          # update C,M,B,inv
          C=self_conv(a); M=float(C.max())
          a0=float(a[0])
          if a0 <= 1e-300:
              # add tiny? break
              # maybe cannot continue
              break
          B=a/a0; B[0]=1.0
          inv=_reciprocal_step(B, inv, n, n_steps=2)
          if (it % recompute_every == recompute_every-1) or (float(np.max(np.abs(inv)))>1e50):
              inv_new=_reciprocal_full(B,n)
              if inv_new is not None:
                  inv=inv_new
          no_work=0
      else:
          # try with larger epsilon? we already ratios list; if no improve, maybe adjust:
          no_work+=1
          if no_work>=6: break
          # optional: rerun with more aggressive ratios? Could reset ratios to more aggressive if early.
          if no_work==2: ratios=[0.98,0.96,0.93,0.9]
          # if no_work==4: ratios=[0.95,0.9,0.85,0.8]
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Now, performance of `_poly_mul` with threshold:
```
def _poly_mul(a,b,L):
    n0=len(a); n1=len(b)
    if n0==0 or n1==0: return np.zeros(L)
    if n0>L: a=a[:L]
    if n1>L: b=b[:L]
    need=len(a)+len(b)-1
    if need <= 0: return zeros
    if need <= 128:
       out=np.convolve(a,b)
       if out.size < L: out=np.pad(out,(0,L-out.size))
       else: out=out[:L]
       return out
    r=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, r)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b, r)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*B, r)
    if out.size < L: out=np.pad(...)
    else: out=out[:L]
    return out
```
We call with arrays length L; need up to 2L-1; r up to 2L next pow2. If L=6962, need=13923, r=16384. Good.

`self_conv(a)` similar but uses length 2n-1. We can do:
```
M=2*n-1; r=1<<(M-1).bit_length(); A=rfft(a,r); c=irfft(A*A,r)[:M]; return np.maximum(c,0)
```
For n small, maybe conv direct? FFT okay.

n=len(a); M=2*n-1; r=1<<(M-1).bit_length(); A=rfft(a,r); W=rfft(w,r); corr=irfft(np.conj(A)*W, r)[:n]; return np.maximum? no, can be negative.
```
This is O(r). Use same r as self? Could compute together to avoid two FFTs? In objective, we compute c and corr; c uses A; corr uses A and W. We compute A once, use for both. In `_obj_log_power_fg`, after softmax a, compute rfft A, c=irfft(A*A). Then power weights w, rfft W, corr=irfft(conj(A)*W). Good, efficient. Implement inline in objective.

In root step exact score, we only need C max; use self_conv. In linear step, we use one poly_mul for deltaB and self_conv for candidate; separate.

Power F/weights from c:
```
def _power_fw(c, q):
    n_c=len(c)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0: return 0.0, np.zeros(n_c)
    x=c/cmax
    if q <= 0: q=2
    # T=sum x^q
    # direct
    xq=np.power(x, q)
    T=float(xq.sum())
    if T <= 0 or not finite:
        # find max index
        idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=zeros; w[idx]=1.0; F=cmax? For homogeneous? If T zero impossible
    G=(T/n_c)**(1.0/q)
    F=cmax*G
    if q > 1:
        xqm1=np.power(x, q-1)
    else: xqm1=np.ones_like(x)
    w=G*xqm1/T
    s=float(np.dot(c,w))
    if s>0 and finite: w = w*(F/s)
    else: ...
    return F,w
```
For q large, x**q underflow but T at least 1 (x=1). np.power(x, q) where x has 0 and q noninteger? x nonnegative; 0^positive ok. If q=64 (int float), fine.

But x may contain values >1 due numerical? c/cmax <=1+epsilon. Clip to 1: `x=np.minimum(x,1.0); x=np.maximum(x,0.0)`. Good.

If q is, say, 64, G=(T/n_c)^(1/64). If T maybe ~1000, G~1.05. F slightly above cmax? For power norm, if many near max, F can exceed cmax? P=Σx^q/L, G=P^{1/q}. Since x≤1, P≤1, G≤1. Wait T=Σx^q ≤L, so T/L≤1, G≤1. F≤cmax. Good. For exact max, F approaches max from below. Score lower approximation? Minimizing F_q (below max) then exact maybe. Okay.

If `F=0` handle.

Now, L-BFGS objective with power: For q high, F close max; for q=8, F maybe average-ish? Could optimize average of c^8. Good.

Flat square root iterative:
```
def _flat_square_root(n):
   b=np.empty(n); b[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): b[i]=b[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   return b
```
This is coefficients of (1-z)^-1/2. For n=10000, loop Python okay. Could vectorize with gammaln but loop fine.

Let's implement a robust `_safe_inv` function:
```
def _safe_reciprocal(B,n):
   if B[0] <= 1e-12: return None
   Bn=B.copy(); Bn[0]=1.0
   inv=_reciprocal_full(Bn,n)
   if inv is None: return None
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(inv)): return None
   maxabs=float(np.max(np.abs(inv)))
   if maxabs > 1e8: # maybe too large
       return None
   return inv
```

Now, implement `_run_log_power_lbfgs` details:
```
def _run_log_power_lbfgs(a0, q, maxfun, maxiter, time_limit=None, gtol=1e-8):
    a0,ok=_normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if not ok: return a0, compute_score(a0)
    n=len(a0)
    base_sc=compute_score(a0)
    a_safe=np.maximum(a0, 1e-30)
    z=np.log(a_safe)
    z=z - z.mean()
    z=np.clip(z, -70, 70)
    global _OBJ_Q
    _OBJ_Q = float(q)
    if not _HAS_SCIPY: return a0, base_sc
    bounds=[(-70.0,70.0)] * n
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_log_power_fg, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':gtol, 'maxls':50})
       z_opt=res.x
       a_opt=_softmax_from_z(z_opt)
       a_opt,ok=_normalize_nonnegative(a_opt)
       sc=compute_score(a_opt)
       if sc < base_sc:
          return a_opt, sc
       else:
          return a0, base_sc
    except Exception as e:
       print('lbfgs error', e)
       return a0, base_sc
```
Need `global` statement inside function? In top-level helper, to assign global `_OBJ_Q`, use `global _OBJ_Q`. Is that allowed? Yes.

Softmax top-level:
```
def _softmax_from_z(z):
   y=z - float(np.mean(z))
   y=np.clip(y, -70, 70)
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
   return e/s
```
In objective, use `y=x - x.mean(); y=np.clip(y,-70,70)` and `a=exp(y)/sum`.

`_obj_log_power_fg` should not call `_softmax_from_z` to avoid extra? It can.
```
def _obj_log_power_fg(x):
   a=_softmax_from_z(x)
   n=len(a)
   M=2*n-1
   r=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,r)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,r)[:M]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   F,w=_power_from_c(c, _OBJ_Q)
   score=2.0*n*F
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,r)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,r)[:n]
   grad=4.0*n*a*(corr - F)
   grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
   # ensure finite
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return score, grad
```
Potential issue: `_power_from_c` uses q; if q not finite? set.

If `F=0`, w zeros, corr zero, grad zero. Good.

Can optimize by using `np.conj(A)`; A is complex. W rfft of real w. Good.

`_normalize_sequence(a)`:
```
def _normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
       return None
    a[np.isnan(a)] = 0.0
    a[np.isposinf(a)] = 0.0
    a[np.isneginf(a)] = 0.0
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a /= s
    return a
```
But if a has negative due clip no. If all zero, uniform.

`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a)
    n=len(a)
    c=_self_conv(a)
    return 2.0*n*float(c.max())
```
Maybe no normalize inside to avoid clipping repeatedly? We'll normalize before.

`_self_conv(a)`:
```
def _self_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 64: # maybe direct
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       M=2*n-1; r=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,r)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,r)[:M]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For n small direct okay.

`_poly_mul_net` as above.

`_power_F_w(c, q)`:
```
def _power_F_w(c, q):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    n_c=len(c)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0 or n_c==0:
       return 0.0, np.zeros(n_c)
    q=float(q)
    if q < 1.5: q=1.5? Use 2
    x=c/cmax
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    if q <= 64.0:
        xq=np.power(x, q)
    else: # log for large, but we won't use >64
    T=float(xq.sum())
    if T <= 0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        w=np.zeros(n_c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; return cmax, w  # F=cmax, weight on max (for q inf)
    G=T ** (1.0/q)
    F=cmax*G
    if q <= 1.0:
       xqm1=np.ones_like(x)
    elif q <= 64.0:
       xqm1=np.power(x, q-1.0)
    else:
       # log compute
       xqm1=np.zeros_like(x); pos=x>0; logx=np.log(x[pos]); xqm1[pos]=np.exp((q-1)*logx)
    w=G*xqm1/T
    s=float(np.dot(c,w))
    if s>0 and np.isfinite(s):
       w = w * (F/s)
    else:
       w=np.zeros(n_c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; F=cmax
    return F,w
```
If q=64, T maybe float. Good.

`_softmax_from_z` as above.

`_obj_power_fg`:
```
def _obj_power_fg(x):
    a=_softmax_from_z(x)
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    r=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,r)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,r)[:M]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    F,w=_power_F_w(c, _OBJ_Q)
    score=2.0*n*F
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,r)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,r)[:n]
    grad=4.0*n*a*(corr - F)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return score, grad
```
If SciPy `fun` with `jac=True` expects tuple; yes. If some versions expect separate? Let's confirm SciPy minimize: If jac is True, fun must return (f, g). If not, error? I think correct. To be safer, we can define two functions `_obj_power_f(x)` and `_obj_power_g(x)` and use separate? That doubles FFT. But compatibility? In recent scipy, `fun` can return tuple if `jac=True`. Use that. If old? likely modern.

Could pass `jac=_obj_power_fg`? No, jac callable expects x; if we set `fun=_obj_power_f`, `jac=_obj_power_g`. But double cost. Maybe provide separate but use cache? Too complex. Use tuple.

If SciPy doesn't support tuple, our code fails. But allowed libraries likely current. The previous program used separate `obj_and_grad` returning tuple with `jac=True`? It did:
```
def obj_and_grad(x): return lse_score_and_grad(x,beta)

```

`_estimate_objective_time`:
```
def _estimate_objective_time(a0, q, samples=5):
    global _OBJ_Q
    _OBJ_Q=float(q)
    a=_normalize_sequence(a0)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-70,70)
    # warmup
    try:
       _obj_power_fg(z)
    except Exception:
       return 0.005
    t=time.monotonic()
    cnt=0
    while cnt<samples:
       try: _obj_power_fg(z)
       except Exception: break
       cnt+=1
    if cnt==0: return 0.005
    return (time.monotonic()-t)/cnt
```
This uses time module.

`_run_log_power_lbfgs`:
```
def _run_log_power_lbfgs(a0, q, maxfun, maxiter, gtol=1e-8):
    a0,b_ok=_normalize_sequence(a0)
    if not b_ok: return a0, np.inf
    n=len(a0); base_sc=_score(a0)
    if not _HAS_SCIPY or n < 16: return a0, base_sc
    global _OBJ_Q
    _OBJ_Q=float(q)
    a_safe=np.maximum(a0, 1e-30)
    z=np.log(a_safe); z-=float(z.mean()); z=np.clip(z,-70,70)
    bounds=[(-70.0,70.0)] for? Need list: `bounds = [(-70.0, 70.0)] * n`.
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_power_fg, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':gtol, 'maxls':30})
       z_opt=res.x
       a_opt=_softmax_from_z(z_opt)
       a_opt=_normalize_sequence(a_opt)
       sc=_score(a_opt)
       if sc < base_sc:
          return a_opt, sc
       return a0, base_sc
    except Exception as e:
       print('LBFGS error:', e, flush=True)
       return a0, base_sc
```
Need `_normalize_sequence` returns array always. Good.

def _linear_root_polish(a0, deadline, max_iters=10000, stage_label='root'):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a0); sc=_score(a)
    best_a=a; best_sc=sc; n=len(a)
    print start
    if n<4: return best_a,best_sc
    C=_self_conv(a); M=float(C.max())
    ratios=[0.99,0.98,0.96,0.93,0.90]
    aggressive_ratios=[0.90,0.85,0.80,0.75]
    betas=[0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]
    recompute_every=8
    inv=None
    B=None
    a0_val=a[0]
    def? no nested.
    # initialize inv
    if a0_val > 1e-20:
       B=a/a0_val; B[0]=1.0
       inv=_safe_reciprocal(B,n)
    if inv is None:
       # try to add tiny a0 to enable?
       a_mod=a.copy(); a_mod[0]=max(a_mod[0], 1e-8); normalize; maybe
       ...
       if still none: return best_a,best_sc
    no_improve=0
    used_aggressive=False
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # if a[0] too small, break or reinit
       a0_val=float(a[0])
       if a0_val <= 1e-20:
           # add tiny
           a[0]=1e-10; a=_normalize_sequence(a); sc=_score(a); update; recompute inv
           if ... break
       # current B
       B=a/a0_val; B[0]=1.0
       improved=False
       active_ratios = aggressive_ratios if (no_improve>=2 and not used_aggressive) else ratios
       for ratio in active_ratios:
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           level=M*ratio
           first=C[:n]
           active = first > level
           if n>1: active[0]=False
           if not np.any(active): continue
           dC=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
           dC[active] = level - first[active]
           denom=a0_val*a0_val
           if denom <= 0: break
           dD=dC/denom
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(dD)): continue
           deltaB=0.5*_poly_mul(inv, dD, n)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(deltaB)): continue
           deltaB[0]=0.0
           delta_max=float(np.max(np.abs(deltaB)))
           if delta_max <= 1e-15: continue
           cap_scale=min(1.0, 0.25/delta_max)
           for beta in betas:
              if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
              step=beta*cap_scale
              if step <=0: continue
              B_new=B + step*deltaB
              B_new[0]=1.0
              a_new=a0_val*B_new
              if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)): continue
              # reject large negatives
              maxa=float(np.max(np.abs(a_new))) if a_new.size else 0.0
              if maxa >0 and float(np.min(a_new)) < -0.5*maxa:
                  a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
                  # maybe if many clipped, skip? compute anyway
              a_try=_normalize_sequence(a_new)
              sc_try=_score(a_try)
              if sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                  best_sc=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); improved=True; break
           if improved: break
       if improved:
           a=best_a.copy()
           # update C M
           C=_self_conv(a); M=float(C.max())
           a0_val=float(a[0])
           if a0_val <= 1e-20:
               # persist by clipping
               a=np.maximum(a,1e-30); a=_normalize_sequence(a); C=...; a0_val=a[0]
           B=a/a0_val; B[0]=1.0
           inv=_reciprocal_update(B, inv, n, 2)
           it%=recompute? after each improved, recompute counter
           improve_count +=1
           if improve_count % recompute_every ==0:
              inv2=_safe_reciprocal(B,n)
              if inv2 is not None: inv=inv2
           no_improve=0
           if best_sc < 1.0: break # impossible
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve>=3 and not used_aggressive:
                used_aggressive=True
                # maybe recompute C/M? already
           if no_improve >= 8:
                break
    return best_a,best_sc
```

`_reciprocal_full` needs care with arrays length. Code:
```
def _reciprocal_full(B, length):
    B=np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if B.size==0: return None
    B0=float(B[0])
    if not np.isfinite(B0) or abs(B0)<1e-300: return None
    b=B.astype(np.float64).copy(); b=b/B0; b[0]=1.0
    inv=np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m=1
    while m < length:
       L=min(2*m,length)
       b_pad=np.zeros(L); b_pad[:m]=b[:m]
       inv_pad=np.zeros(L); inv_pad[:m]=inv
       t=_poly_mul(b_pad, inv_pad, L)
       rhs=np.zeros(L); rhs[0]=2.0
       rhs -= t
       inv=_poly_mul(inv_pad, rhs, L)
       m=L
    if B0 != 1.0:
       inv = inv / B0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(inv)): return None
    return inv
```
If B0 very small (a0 small), b=B/B0 coefficients huge; inverse may huge. safe threshold.

def _reciprocal_update(B, inv, length, n_steps):
    B=np.asarray(B,float).copy()
    inv=np.asarray(inv,float)[:length].copy()
    for _ in range(n_steps):
       t=_poly_mul(B, inv, length)
       rhs=np.zeros(length); rhs[0]=2.0; rhs-=t
       inv=_poly_mul(inv, rhs, length)
    if not finite: return None
    return inv
```

This handles nan/inf. Then clip.

```
def _normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       n=max(1,a.size)
       return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)/n
    return a / s
```
If a all negative, clipped to 0, sum 0 -> uniform.

`_score`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=_normalize_sequence(a)
    n=len(a)
    c=_self_conv(a)
    return 2.0*n*float(c.max()) if n else np.inf
```

def propose_candidate():
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    parent_arr = None
    try:
       p = height_sequence_1
       if p is not None:
          p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if p.size >= 64:
             if p.size > 20000:
                x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
                x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,20000)
                p=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
             parent_arr=_normalize_sequence(p)
    except Exception as e:
       print('parent load error', e, flush=True)
    n_default=6962
    if parent_arr is None:
       n=n_default
       best_a=_flat_square_root_n(n)
    else:
       best_a=parent_arr
       n=len(best_a)
    best_sc=_score(best_a)
    print('initial score', best_sc)
    # alternatives
    alts=[]
    alts.append(_flat_square_root_n(n))
    x=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    alts.append(_normalize_sequence(1.0/np.sqrt(x)))
    if len(best_a)==n:
       pert=best_a*(1.0+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)); alts.append(_normalize_sequence(pert))
    for alt in alts:
       sc=_score(alt)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=alt.copy(); print('alt improved')
    # quick root stage1
    root_limit1 = t0 + 350.0
    a1,sc1=_linear_root_polish(best_a, deadline=min(root_limit1, deadline-1500? no), label='root1')
```

```
def _linear_root_polish(a0, deadline, label='root'):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    n = len(a)
    print(f"{label}: start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
    if n < 16: return best_a,best_sc
    C = _self_conv(a)
    M = float(C.max())
    stage_ratios = [...]
    betas = ...
    stage=0; fail=0; inv=None
    a0v=float(a[0])
    # ensure a0
    if a0v <= 1e-12:
        a_mod=a.copy(); a_mod[0]=1e-8
        a_mod=_normalize_sequence(a_mod)
        sc_mod=_score(a_mod)
        if sc_mod < best_sc:
           best_a=a_mod.copy(); best_sc=sc_mod
        a=a_mod
        C=_self_conv(a); M=float(C.max()); a0v=float(a[0])
    B=a/a0v; B[0]=1.0
    inv=_safe_reciprocal(B,n)
    if inv is None:
       print(f"{label}: inverse unstable, returning", flush=True)
       return best_a,best_sc
    improve_count=0
    for it in range(100000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        a0v=float(a[0])
        if a0v <= 1e-12:
            break
        B=a/a0v; B[0]=1.0
        improved=False
        ratios=stage_ratios[min(stage, len(stage_ratios)-1)]
        for ratio in ratios:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
            level=M*ratio
            first=C[:n]
            active=first > level
            if n > 1:
                active[0]=False
            if not np.any(active): continue
            dC=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            dC[active]=level - first[active]
            denom=a0v*a0v
            if denom <=0 or not np.isfinite(denom): break
            dD=dC/denom
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(dD)): continue
            deltaB=0.5*_poly_mul(inv,dD,n)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(deltaB)): continue
            deltaB[0]=0.0
            delta_max=float(np.max(np.abs(deltaB)))
            if delta_max <= 1e-15: continue
            cap_scale=min(1.0, 0.4/(delta_max + 1e-300))
            for beta in betas:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                step=beta*cap_scale
                if step <=0: continue
                B_new=B + step*deltaB
                B_new[0]=1.0
                a_new=a0v*B_new
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)): continue
                # if very clipped, still try
                if float(np.min(a_new)) < -0.75*max(1e-300, float(np.max(a_new))):
                    # maybe reject if too negative to avoid bad clipping
                    pass? We can still clip; but reject if min < -1.0 max?
                a_try=_normalize_sequence(a_new)
                sc_try=_score(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); improved=True; break
            if improved: break
        if improved:
            a=best_a.copy()
            C=_self_conv(a)
            M=float(C.max())
            a0v=float(a[0])
            if a0v <= 1e-12:
                a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
                a[0]=1e-8; a=_normalize_sequence(a); C=_self_conv(a); M=float(C.max()); a0v=float(a[0])
            B=a/a0v; B[0]=1.0
            inv_up=_reciprocal_update(B, inv, n, 2)
            if inv_up is not None:
                inv=inv_up
            improve_count += 1
            if improve_count % 5 == 0 or (improve_count % 20 ==0 and float(np.max(np.abs(inv)))>1e6):
                inv2=_safe_reciprocal(B,n)
                if inv2 is not None:
                    inv=inv2
                else:
                    break
            if improve_count % 10 == 0:
                print(f"{label}: improved to {best_sc:.8f} (iter {it})", flush=True)
            stage=0
            fail=0
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail == 1:
                stage = min(stage+1, len(stage_ratios)-1)
            if fail >= 3:
                break
    print(f"{label}: final score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Now, one more thought: L-BFGS with power objective could find sequence with lower surrogate but exact score worse; our `run_lbfgs` returns `a0, base_sc` if `res.x` exact not better. But if it stops by _TimeStop, it returns input though maybe partial would improve. We don't have partial. Fine.

Now, final code. Let's compose final answer. Ensure no comments? okay.

Double-check top-level helpers all defined before use? Python resolves at call, order okay if defined before propose called. We'll define in order.

```python
"""
Optimization for minimizing 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2 over nonnegative
sequences.  The main optimizer combines:
  * a Newton-style "targeted convolution capping" step in the polynomial
    square-root space of A(z)^2, which directly attacks the active peaks;
  * log-softmax L-BFGS on a smooth power-norm surrogate of the max-convolution
    objective;
  * repeated exact score checks and final polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False

_OBJ_Q = 16.0
_TIME_STOP_AT = 1e100


class _TimeStop(Exception):
    pass


def _normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 128:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        m = 2 * n - 1
        r = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, r)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, r)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score(a):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _self_conv(a)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())


def _poly_mul(a, b, L):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0 or L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size > L:
        a = a[:L]
    if b.size > L:
        b = b[:L]
    need = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if need <= 0:
        return np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if need <= 128:
        out = np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")
        if out.size < L:
            out = np.pad(out, (0, L - out.size))
        else:
            out = out[:L]
        return np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    r = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, r)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, r)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * B, r)
    if out.size < L:
        out = np.pad(out, (0, L - out.size))
    else:
        out = out[:L]
    return np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)


def _reciprocal_full(B, length):
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if B.size == 0 or length <= 0:
        return None
    B0 = float(B[0])
    if not np.isfinite(B0) or abs(B0) < 1e-300:
        return None
    b = B.astype(np.float64).copy()
    b = b / B0
    b[0] = 1.0
    inv = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 1
    while m < length:
        L = min(2 * m, length)
        b_pad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        b_pad[:m] = b[:m]
        inv_pad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        inv_pad[:m] = inv
        t = _poly_mul(b_pad, inv_pad, L)
        rhs = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        rhs[0] = 2.0
        rhs -= t
        inv = _poly_mul(inv_pad, rhs, L)
        m = L
    if B0 != 1.0:
        inv = inv / B0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(inv)):
        return None
    return inv


def _reciprocal_update(B, inv, length, n_steps):
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    inv = np.asarray(inv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if B.size == 0 or inv.size == 0 or length <= 0:
        return None
    B = B[:length]
    inv = inv[:length]
    if inv.size < length:
        inv_full = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
        inv_full[:inv.size] = inv
        inv = inv_full
    for _ in range(max(0, int(n_steps))):
        t = _poly_mul(B, inv, length)
        rhs = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
        rhs[0] = 2.0
        rhs -= t
        inv = _poly_mul(inv, rhs, length)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(inv)):
        return None
    return inv


def _safe_reciprocal(B, length):
    inv = _reciprocal_full(B, length)
    if inv is None:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(inv)):
        return None
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(inv)))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx > 1e10:
        return None
    return inv


def _power_F_w(c, q):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    nc = len(c)
    if nc == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(nc, dtype=np.float64)
    q = float(q)
    if not np.isfinite(q) or q < 2.0:
        q = 2.0
    x = c / cmax
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    xq = np.power(x, q)
    T = float(xq.sum())
    if T <= 0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        w = np.zeros(nc, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return cmax, w
    G = T ** (1.0 / q)
    F = cmax * G
    if q <= 1.0:
        xqm1 = np.ones_like(x)
    else:
        xqm1 = np.power(x, q - 1.0)
    w = G * xqm1 / T
    s = float(np.dot(c, w))
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
        w = w * (F / s)
    else:
        w = np.zeros(nc, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        F = cmax
    return float(F), w


def _softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = z - float(np.mean(z))
    y = np.clip(y, -70.0, 70.0)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _obj_power_fg(x):
    if time.monotonic() > _TIME_STOP_AT:
        raise _TimeStop()

    a = _softmax_from_z(x)
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    r = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, r)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, r)[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)

    F, w = _power_F_w(c, _OBJ_Q)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * F

    W = np.fft.rfft(w, r)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, r)[:n]
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (corr - F)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    return score, grad


def _estimate_objective_time(a0, q, samples=5):
    global _OBJ_Q, _TIME_STOP_AT
    old_stop = _TIME_STOP_AT
    old_q = _OBJ_Q
    _TIME_STOP_AT = 1e100
    _OBJ_Q = float(q)
    try:
        a = _normalize_sequence(a0)
        a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-30)
        z = np.log(a_safe)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)
        try:
            _obj_power_fg(z)
        except Exception:
            return 0.005
        t = time.monotonic()
        cnt = 0
        while cnt < max(1, int(samples)):
            try:
                _obj_power_fg(z)
            except Exception:
                break
            cnt += 1
        if cnt == 0:
            return 0.005
        return (time.monotonic() - t) / float(cnt)
    finally:
        _TIME_STOP_AT = old_stop
        _OBJ_Q = old_q


def _run_log_power_lbfgs(a0, q, maxfun, maxiter, stop_time, gtol=1e-8):
    a0 = _normalize_sequence(a0)
    base_sc = _score(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    if not _HAS_SCIPY or n < 16:
        return a0, base_sc

    global _OBJ_Q, _TIME_STOP_AT
    old_q = _OBJ_Q
    old_stop = _TIME_STOP_AT
    _OBJ_Q = float(q)
    _TIME_STOP_AT = float(stop_time)

    a_safe = np.maximum(a0, 1e-30)
    z = np.log(a_safe)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -70.0, 70.0)
    bounds = [(-70.0, 70.0)] * n

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_power_fg,
            z,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": gtol,
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        z_opt = res.x
        a_opt = _softmax_from_z(z_opt)
        a_opt = _normalize_sequence(a_opt)
        sc = _score(a_opt)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base_sc:
            return a_opt, float(sc)
        return a0, float(base_sc)
    except _TimeStop:
        print("  LBFGS: stop by internal time limit", flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("  LBFGS error:", e, flush=True)
    finally:
        _TIME_STOP_AT = old_stop
        _OBJ_Q = old_q

    return a0, float(base_sc)


def _apply_log_power_schedule(a0, deadline):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(best_a)
    cur_a = best_a.copy()
    print(f"  LBFGS schedule start: {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    t_est = _estimate_objective_time(cur_a, 16.0, 5)
    t_est = max(1e-5, float(t_est))

    q_configs = [
        (8.0, 8000),
        (16.0, 20000),
        (32.0, 12000),
        (64.0, 8000),
    ]

    scale_n = max(0.5, 7000.0 / max(1, len(a)))

    for q, cap in q_configs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now
        if rem < 120.0:
            break
        allowed = min(350.0, rem - 90.0)
        if allowed < 60.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(allowed * 0.7 / t_est)
        maxfun = int(max(2000, min(maxfun, cap * scale_n)))
        stop_time = min(deadline - 45.0, now + allowed * 1.25 + 30.0)
        print(
            f"    q={q:g}, maxfun={maxfun}, allowed~{allowed:.0f}s",
            flush=True,
        )
        a_new, sc_new = _run_log_power_lbfgs(
            cur_a, q, maxfun, maxiter=2500, stop_time=stop_time, gtol=1e-8
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc * 1.05:
            cur_a = a_new.copy()
        else:
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
        print(f"    after q={q:g}: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    print(f"  LBFGS schedule end: {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
    return best_a, best_sc


def _flat_square_root_n(n):
    b = np.empty(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, len(b)):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return _normalize_sequence(b)


def _linear_root_polish(a0, deadline, label="root"):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(best_a)
    n = len(a)
    print(f"{label}: start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    if n < 16:
        return best_a, best_sc

    C = _self_conv(a)
    M = float(C.max())

    stage_ratios = [
        [0.995, 0.99, 0.985, 0.98],
        [0.98, 0.97, 0.95, 0.93],
        [0.95, 0.92, 0.90, 0.87],
        [0.90, 0.85, 0.80, 0.75],
    ]
    betas = [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]

    stage = 0
    fail = 0
    improve_count = 0
    inv = None

    a0v = float(a[0])
    if a0v <= 1e-12:
        a_mod = a.copy()
        a_mod[0] = 1e-8
        a_mod = _normalize_sequence(a_mod)
        sc_mod = _score(a_mod)
        if sc_mod < best_sc:
            best_a = a_mod.copy()
            best_sc = sc_mod
        a = a_mod.copy()
        C = _self_conv(a)
        M = float(C.max())
        a0v = float(a[0])

    if a0v <= 1e-12:
        print(f"{label}: a0 too small, aborting", flush=True)
        return best_a, best_sc

    B = a / a0v
    B[0] = 1.0
    inv = _safe_reciprocal(B, n)
    if inv is None:
        print(f"{label}: reciprocal unstable, aborting", flush=True)
        return best_a, best_sc

    for it in range(100000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        a0v = float(a[0])
        if a0v <= 1e-12:
            break

        B = a / a0v
        B[0] = 1.0
        improved = False
        ratios = stage_ratios[min(stage, len(stage_ratios) - 1)]

        for ratio in ratios:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            level = M * ratio
            first = C[:n]
            active = first > level
            if n > 1:
                active[0] = False
            if not np.any(active):
                continue

            dC = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            dC[active] = level - first[active]
            denom = a0v * a0v
            if denom <= 0 or not np.isfinite(denom):
                break

            dD = dC / denom
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(dD)):
                continue
            mx_dd = float(np.max(np.abs(dD)))
            if not np.isfinite(mx_dd) or mx_dd <= 0:
                continue

            deltaB = 0.5 * _poly_mul(inv, dD, n)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(deltaB)):
                continue
            deltaB[0] = 0.0
            delta_max = float(np.max(np.abs(deltaB)))
            if not np.isfinite(delta_max) or delta_max <= 1e-15:
                continue

            cap_scale = min(1.0, 0.4 / max(1e-300, delta_max))

            for beta in betas:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                step = beta * cap_scale
                if step <= 0 or not np.isfinite(step):
                    continue

                B_new = B + step * deltaB
                B_new[0] = 1.0
                a_new = a0v * B_new
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
                    continue

                a_try = _normalize_sequence(a_new)
                sc_try = _score(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = float(sc_try)
                    best_a = a_try.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            a = best_a.copy()
            C = _self_conv(a)
            M = float(C.max())
            a0v = float(a[0])
            if a0v <= 1e-12:
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                a[0] = 1e-8
                a = _normalize_sequence(a)
                C = _self_conv(a)
                M = float(C.max())
                a0v = float(a[0])
                if a0v <= 1e-12:
                    break

            B = a / a0v
            B[0] = 1.0
            inv_up = _reciprocal_update(B, inv, n, 2)
            if inv_up is not None:
                inv = inv_up

            improve_count += 1
            if improve_count % 5 == 0:
                inv2 = _safe_reciprocal(B, n)
                if inv2 is not None:
                    inv = inv2
                else:
                    break
            if improve_count % 10 == 0:
                print(f"{label}: improved to {best_sc:.8f} (iter {it})", flush=True)

            stage = 0
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail == 1:
                stage = min(stage + 1, len(stage_ratios) - 1)
            if fail >= 3:
                break

    print(f"{label}: final score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate():
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(180.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 64:
                if p.size > 20000:
                    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
                    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 20000)
                    p = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
                parent_arr = _normalize_sequence(p)
    except Exception as e:
        print("parent load issue:", e, flush=True)

    n_default = 6962
    if parent_arr is None:
        n = n_default
        best_a = _flat_square_root_n(n)
    else:
        best_a = parent_arr.copy()
        n = len(best_a)

    best_sc = _score(best_a)
    print(f"initial: n={n}, score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    alts = []
    alts.append(_flat_square_root_n(n))
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    alts.append(_normalize_sequence(1.0 / np.sqrt(x)))
    if len(best_a) == n:
        pert = best_a * (1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        alts.append(_normalize_sequence(pert))

    for alt in alts:
        sc = _score(alt)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = alt.copy()
            print(f"alternative improved to {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Primary polynomial peak-cap polish.
    root1_deadline = min(t0 + 350.0, deadline - 1500.0)
    if time.monotonic() < root1_deadline - 30.0:
        a1, sc1 = _linear_root_polish(best_a, root1_deadline, "root1")
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc1)
            best_a = a1.copy()

    # Smooth log-softmax power-norm polishing.
    lbfgs_deadline = min(t0 + 1250.0, deadline - 900.0)
    if time.monotonic() < lbfgs_deadline - 120.0:
        a2, sc2 = _apply_log_power_schedule(best_a, lbfgs_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc2)
            best_a = a2.copy()

    # Final exact peak polish.
    final_deadline = deadline - 30.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 55.0:
        a3, sc3 = _linear_root_polish(best_a, final_deadline, "root2")
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc3)
            best_a = a3.copy()

    # If enough time remains and the score is still a bit above target, run a
    # very short high-q non-smooth refinement and then one final root polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0 and best_sc > 1.502:
        short_stop = min(deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        a4, sc4 = _run_log_power_lbfgs(
            best_a, 64.0, 5000, maxiter=1000, stop_time=short_stop, gtol=1e-9
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc4)
            best_a = a4.copy()
        final_small = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
        if time.monotonic() < final_small - 20.0:
            a5, sc5 = _linear_root_polish(best_a, final_small, "root3")
            if np.isfinite(sc5) and sc5 < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc5)
                best_a = a5.copy()

    out_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_a = np.where(np.isfinite(out_a), out_a, 0.0)
    out_a = np.clip(out_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out_sum = float(out_a.sum())
    if out_sum <= 0.0:
        out_a = np.ones(max(1, len(out_a)), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(out_a))

    out_list = [float(v) for v in out_a]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print(f"FINAL: internal={best_sc:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, len={len(out_list)}",
          flush=True)
    return out_list
```
